     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// hookConfig represents the .github/hooks/copilot-setup.json structure
     6→type hookConfig struct {
     7→	Version int                    `json:"version"`
     8→	Hooks   map[string][]hookEntry `json:"hooks"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// hookEntry represents a single hook command entry
    12→type hookEntry struct {
    13→	Type       string `json:"type"`
    14→	Bash       string `json:"bash"`
    15→	TimeoutSec int    `json:"timeoutSec,omitempty"`
    16→}
    17→
    18→// --- Hook stdin types (from actual Copilot CLI v0.0.411 capture) ---
    19→//
    20→// Common fields: only "timestamp" (unix milliseconds) and "cwd" are present in all hooks.
    21→// "sessionId" and "transcriptPath" are only present in the agentStop hook.
    22→
    23→// hookBase contains the only fields present in ALL Copilot hook stdin JSON.
    24→type hookBase struct {
    25→	Timestamp int64  `json:"timestamp"`
    26→	Cwd       string `json:"cwd"`
    27→}
    28→
    29→// sessionStartRaw is the stdin JSON for the sessionStart hook.
    30→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465283476,"cwd":"/path","source":"new","initialPrompt":"..."}
    31→type sessionStartRaw struct {
    32→	hookBase
    33→
    34→	Source        string `json:"source,omitempty"`
    35→	InitialPrompt string `json:"initialPrompt,omitempty"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// sessionEndRaw is the stdin JSON for the sessionEnd hook.
    39→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465290000,"cwd":"/path","reason":"complete"}
    40→type sessionEndRaw struct {
    41→	hookBase
    42→
    43→	Reason string `json:"reason,omitempty"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// userPromptRaw is the stdin JSON for the userPromptSubmitted hook.
    47→// Example: {"timestamp":1771465282293,"cwd":"/path","prompt":"..."}
    48→type userPromptRaw struct {
    49→	hookBase
    50→
    51→	Prompt string `json:"prompt,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// agentStopRaw is the stdin JSON for the agentStop hook.
    55→// This is the only hook that includes sessionId and transcriptPath.
    56→// Example: {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","sessionId":"uuid","transcriptPath":"/path/events.jsonl","stopReason":"end_turn"}
    57→type agentStopRaw struct {
    58→	hookBase
    59→
    60→	SessionID      string `json:"sessionId"`
    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcriptPath"`
    62→	StopReason     string `json:"stopReason,omitempty"`
    63→}
    64→
    65→// subagentStopRaw is the stdin JSON for the subagentStop hook.
    66→// Assumed to follow agentStop format (not yet captured in the wild).
    67→type subagentStopRaw struct {
    68→	hookBase
    69→
    70→	SessionID      string `json:"sessionId,omitempty"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcriptPath,omitempty"`
    72→}
    73→
    74→// toolUseRaw is the stdin JSON for preToolUse/postToolUse hooks.
    75→// Note: toolArgs is a JSON string in preToolUse but an object in postToolUse.
    76→// Example (pre):  {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","toolName":"view","toolArgs":"{\"path\":\"/foo\"}"}
    77→// Example (post): {"timestamp":...,"cwd":"/path","toolName":"view","toolArgs":{"path":"/foo"},"toolResult":{...}}
    78→type toolUseRaw struct {
    79→	hookBase
    80→
    81→	ToolName   string          `json:"toolName"`
    82→	ToolArgs   json.RawMessage `json:"toolArgs,omitempty"`
    83→	ToolResult json.RawMessage `json:"toolResult,omitempty"`
    84→}
    85→
    86→// --- events.jsonl types (session transcript format) ---
    87→
    88→// Event represents a single line in a Copilot events.jsonl transcript.
    89→type Event struct {
    90→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
    91→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
    92→	ID        string          `json:"id"`
    93→	Timestamp string          `json:"timestamp"`
    94→	ParentID  *string         `json:"parentId"`
    95→}
    96→
    97→// Event type constants for events.jsonl
    98→//
    99→// Known types not listed here (unused but documented):
   100→//
   101→//	session.end, session.mode_changed, session.model_change,
   102→//	assistant.turn_start, assistant.turn_end, tool.execution_complete
   103→const (
   104→	EventUserMessage      = "user.message"
   105→	EventAssistantMessage = "assistant.message"
   106→	EventToolExecStart    = "tool.execution_start"
   107→)
   108→
   109→// userMessageData is the data field for user.message events.
   110→type userMessageData struct {
   111→	Content            string `json:"content"`
   112→	TransformedContent string `json:"transformedContent"`
   113→}
   114→
   115→// assistantMessageData is the data field for assistant.message events.
   116→type assistantMessageData struct {
   117→	MessageID    string        `json:"messageId"`
   118→	Content      string        `json:"content"`
   119→	ToolRequests []toolRequest `json:"toolRequests"`
   120→}
   121→
   122→// toolRequest represents a tool call request within an assistant.message event.
   123→type toolRequest struct {
   124→	ToolCallID string         `json:"toolCallId"`
   125→	Name       string         `json:"name"`
   126→	Arguments  map[string]any `json:"arguments"`
   127→	Type       string         `json:"type"`
   128→}
   129→
   130→// toolExecStartData is the data field for tool.execution_start events.
   131→type toolExecStartData struct {
   132→	ToolCallID string         `json:"toolCallId"`
   133→	ToolName   string         `json:"toolName"`
   134→	Arguments  map[string]any `json:"arguments"`
   135→}
   136→
   137→// Tool names used in GitHub Copilot that modify files
   138→const (
   139→	ToolEdit   = "edit"
   140→	ToolCreate = "create"
   141→)
   142→
   143→// fileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in GitHub Copilot
   144→var fileModificationTools = []string{
   145→	ToolEdit,
   146→	ToolCreate,
   147→}

     1→package copilot
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"time"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions
    13→var _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    14→
    15→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    16→func (c *CopilotAgent) HookNames() []string {
    17→	return []string{
    18→		HookNameSessionStart,
    19→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    20→		HookNameUserPromptSubmitted,
    21→		HookNameAgentStop,
    22→		HookNameSubagentStop,
    23→		HookNamePreToolUse,
    24→		HookNamePostToolUse,
    25→		HookNameErrorOccurred,
    26→	}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ParseHookEvent translates a Copilot hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    30→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    31→//
    32→// Note: In Copilot CLI, only agentStop provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
    33→// Other hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmitted, sessionEnd) do not include
    34→// these fields, so the resulting Event will have empty SessionID/SessionRef.
    35→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameAgentStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred:
    48→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    49→	default:
    50→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    55→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    56→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return data, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript into chunks.
    64→func (c *CopilotAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    65→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→	return chunks, nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// ReassembleTranscript combines JSONL chunks back into a single transcript.
    73→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    74→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    75→}
    76→
    77→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    78→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    79→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	return extractPromptsFromData(data, fromOffset)
    85→}
    86→
    87→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    88→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    89→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    90→	if err != nil {
    91→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    92→	}
    93→	return extractLastAssistantMessage(data), nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    97→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    98→	if path == "" {
    99→		return 0, nil
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   105→			return 0, nil
   106→		}
   107→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return countLines(data), nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
   114→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   115→	if path == "" {
   116→		return nil, 0, nil
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   120→	if readErr != nil {
   121→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   122→			return nil, 0, nil
   123→		}
   124→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	if len(data) == 0 {
   128→		return nil, 0, nil
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	totalLines := countLines(data)
   132→	extractedFiles := extractModifiedFilesFromLines(data, startOffset)
   133→
   134→	return extractedFiles, totalLines, nil
   135→}
   136→
   137→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   138→
   139→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   140→// Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   141→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). The session is initialized later
   142→// when agentStop fires with the actual sessionId.
   143→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   144→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		return nil, err
   147→	}
   148→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   149→}
   150→
   151→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   152→// Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   153→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). The lifecycle dispatcher requires sessionId
   154→// for TurnStart to capture pre-prompt state and initialize the session, but copilot
   155→// only provides this in agentStop. The prompt text is recovered from the transcript
   156→// during TurnEnd processing.
   157→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   158→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return nil, err
   161→	}
   162→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   163→}
   164→
   165→// parseTurnEnd handles the agentStop hook.
   166→// This is the only hook that provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
   167→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   168→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return nil, err
   171→	}
   172→	return &agent.Event{
   173→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   174→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   175→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   176→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   177→	}, nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→// parseSessionEnd handles the sessionEnd hook.
   181→// Copilot's sessionEnd stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   182→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). Without sessionId, the lifecycle
   183→// dispatcher cannot mark the correct session as ended.
   184→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   185→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionEndRaw](stdin)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return nil, err
   188→	}
   189→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   190→}
   191→
   192→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   193→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   194→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, err
   197→	}
   198→	return &agent.Event{
   199→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   200→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   201→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   202→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   203→	}, nil
   204→}
   205→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import "time"
     4→
     5→// HookType represents agent lifecycle events
     6→type HookType string
     7→
     8→const (
     9→	HookSessionStart     HookType = "session_start"
    10→	HookSessionEnd       HookType = "session_end"
    11→	HookUserPromptSubmit HookType = "user_prompt_submit"
    12→	HookStop             HookType = "stop"
    13→	HookPreToolUse       HookType = "pre_tool_use"
    14→	HookPostToolUse      HookType = "post_tool_use"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// HookInput contains normalized data from hook callbacks
    18→type HookInput struct {
    19→	HookType  HookType
    20→	SessionID string
    21→	// SessionRef is an agent-specific session reference (file path, db key, etc.)
    22→	SessionRef string
    23→	Timestamp  time.Time
    24→
    25→	// UserPrompt is the user's prompt text (from UserPromptSubmit hooks)
    26→	UserPrompt string
    27→
    28→	// Tool-specific fields (PreToolUse/PostToolUse)
    29→	ToolName     string
    30→	ToolUseID    string
    31→	ToolInput    []byte // Raw JSON
    32→	ToolResponse []byte // Raw JSON (PostToolUse only)
    33→
    34→	// RawData preserves agent-specific data for extension
    35→	RawData map[string]interface{}
    36→}
    37→
    38→// SessionChange represents detected session activity (for FileWatcher)
    39→type SessionChange struct {
    40→	SessionID  string
    41→	SessionRef string
    42→	EventType  HookType
    43→	Timestamp  time.Time
    44→}
    45→
    46→// TokenUsage represents aggregated token usage for a checkpoint.
    47→// This is agent-agnostic and can be populated by any agent that tracks token usage.
    48→type TokenUsage struct {
    49→	// InputTokens is the number of input tokens (fresh, not from cache)
    50→	InputTokens int `json:"input_tokens"`
    51→	// CacheCreationTokens is tokens written to cache (billable at cache write rate)
    52→	CacheCreationTokens int `json:"cache_creation_tokens"`
    53→	// CacheReadTokens is tokens read from cache (discounted rate)
    54→	CacheReadTokens int `json:"cache_read_tokens"`
    55→	// OutputTokens is the number of output tokens generated
    56→	OutputTokens int `json:"output_tokens"`
    57→	// APICallCount is the number of API calls made
    58→	APICallCount int `json:"api_call_count"`
    59→	// SubagentTokens contains token usage from spawned subagents (if any)
    60→	SubagentTokens *TokenUsage `json:"subagent_tokens,omitempty"`
    61→}
    62→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"errors"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// EventType represents a normalized lifecycle event from any agent.
    12→// Agents translate their native hooks into these event types via ParseHookEvent.
    13→type EventType int
    14→
    15→const (
    16→	// SessionStart indicates the agent session has begun.
    17→	SessionStart EventType = iota + 1
    18→
    19→	// TurnStart indicates the user submitted a prompt and the agent is about to work.
    20→	TurnStart
    21→
    22→	// TurnEnd indicates the agent finished responding to a prompt.
    23→	TurnEnd
    24→
    25→	// Compaction indicates the agent is about to compress its context window.
    26→	// This triggers the same save logic as TurnEnd but also resets the transcript offset.
    27→	Compaction
    28→
    29→	// SessionEnd indicates the session has been terminated.
    30→	SessionEnd
    31→
    32→	// SubagentStart indicates a subagent (task) has been spawned.
    33→	SubagentStart
    34→
    35→	// SubagentEnd indicates a subagent (task) has completed.
    36→	SubagentEnd
    37→)
    38→
    39→// String returns a human-readable name for the event type.
    40→func (e EventType) String() string {
    41→	switch e {
    42→	case SessionStart:
    43→		return "SessionStart"
    44→	case TurnStart:
    45→		return "TurnStart"
    46→	case TurnEnd:
    47→		return "TurnEnd"
    48→	case Compaction:
    49→		return "Compaction"
    50→	case SessionEnd:
    51→		return "SessionEnd"
    52→	case SubagentStart:
    53→		return "SubagentStart"
    54→	case SubagentEnd:
    55→		return "SubagentEnd"
    56→	default:
    57→		return "Unknown"
    58→	}
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Event is a normalized lifecycle event produced by an agent's ParseHookEvent method.
    62→// The framework dispatcher uses these events to drive checkpoint/session lifecycle actions.
    63→type Event struct {
    64→	// Type is the kind of lifecycle event.
    65→	Type EventType
    66→
    67→	// SessionID identifies the agent session.
    68→	SessionID string
    69→
    70→	// PreviousSessionID is non-empty when this event represents a session continuation
    71→	// or handoff (e.g., Claude starting a new session ID after exiting plan mode).
    72→	PreviousSessionID string
    73→
    74→	// SessionRef is an agent-specific reference to the transcript (typically a file path).
    75→	SessionRef string
    76→
    77→	// Prompt is the user's prompt text (populated on TurnStart events).
    78→	Prompt string
    79→
    80→	// Timestamp is when the event occurred.
    81→	Timestamp time.Time
    82→
    83→	// ToolUseID identifies the tool invocation (for SubagentStart/SubagentEnd events).
    84→	ToolUseID string
    85→
    86→	// SubagentID identifies the subagent instance (for SubagentEnd events).
    87→	SubagentID string
    88→
    89→	// ToolInput is the raw tool input JSON (for subagent type/description extraction).
    90→	ToolInput json.RawMessage
    91→
    92→	// ResponseMessage is an optional message to display to the user via the agent.
    93→	ResponseMessage string
    94→
    95→	// Metadata holds agent-specific state that the framework stores and makes available
    96→	// on subsequent events. Examples: Pi's activeLeafId, Cursor's is_background_agent.
    97→	Metadata map[string]string
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ReadAndParseHookInput reads all bytes from stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   101→// This is a shared helper for agent ParseHookEvent implementations.
   102→func ReadAndParseHookInput[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   103→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→	if len(data) == 0 {
   108→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   109→	}
   110→	var result T
   111→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   112→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   113→	}
   114→	return &result, nil
   115→}
   116→

     1→// Package copilot implements the Agent interface for GitHub Copilot CLI.
     2→package copilot
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    18→func init() {
    19→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameCopilot, NewCopilotAgent)
    20→}
    21→
    22→// CopilotAgent implements the Agent interface for GitHub Copilot CLI.
    23→//
    24→//nolint:revive // CopilotAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    25→type CopilotAgent struct{}
    26→
    27→// NewCopilotAgent creates a new CopilotAgent.
    28→func NewCopilotAgent() agent.Agent {
    29→	return &CopilotAgent{}
    30→}
    31→
    32→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    33→func (c *CopilotAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    34→	return agent.AgentNameCopilot
    35→}
    36→
    37→// Type returns the agent type identifier.
    38→func (c *CopilotAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {
    39→	return agent.AgentTypeCopilot
    40→}
    41→
    42→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    43→func (c *CopilotAgent) Description() string {
    44→	return "GitHub Copilot - GitHub's AI coding assistant"
    45→}
    46→
    47→// IsPreview returns true as the Copilot integration is in preview.
    48→func (c *CopilotAgent) IsPreview() bool { return true }
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if GitHub Copilot is configured in the repository.
    51→func (c *CopilotAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		repoRoot = "."
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Check if our hooks are installed
    58→	if c.AreHooksInstalled() {
    59→		return true, nil
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Check for .github/hooks directory with copilot config
    63→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
    64→	if _, err := os.Stat(configPath); err == nil {
    65→		return true, nil
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	return false, nil
    69→}
    70→
    71→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Copilot's hook config file.
    72→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    73→	return filepath.Join(".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
    74→}
    75→
    76→// SupportsHooks returns true as GitHub Copilot supports lifecycle hooks.
    77→func (c *CopilotAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    78→	return true
    79→}
    80→
    81→// ParseHookInput parses Copilot hook input from stdin.
    82→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    83→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	if len(data) == 0 {
    89→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    93→		HookType:  hookType,
    94→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    95→		RawData:   make(map[string]any),
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	switch hookType {
    99→	case agent.HookSessionStart:
   100→		var raw sessionStartRaw
   101→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   102→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   103→		}
   104→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   105→		input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   106→
   107→	case agent.HookSessionEnd:
   108→		var raw sessionEndRaw
   109→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   110→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   111→		}
   112→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   113→		input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   114→
   115→	case agent.HookStop:
   116→		// agentStop is the only hook with sessionId and transcriptPath
   117→		var raw agentStopRaw
   118→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   119→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   120→		}
   121→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   122→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   123→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   124→		input.RawData["stopReason"] = raw.StopReason
   125→
   126→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   127→		var raw userPromptRaw
   128→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   129→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   130→		}
   131→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   132→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   133→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   134→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   135→		}
   136→
   137→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   138→		var raw toolUseRaw
   139→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   141→		}
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolArgs
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResult
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return input, nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   154→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   155→	return input.SessionID
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Copilot uses.
   159→// .github is a shared directory (not solely ours), so we don't protect it.
   160→func (c *CopilotAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{} }
   161→
   162→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Copilot stores session transcripts.
   163→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".copilot", "session-state"), nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Copilot session file.
   177→func (c *CopilotAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   178→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID, "events.jsonl")
   179→}
   180→
   181→// ReadSession reads a session from Copilot's storage.
   182→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   183→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   184→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   193→		SessionID:  input.SessionID,
   194→		AgentName:  c.Name(),
   195→		SessionRef: input.SessionRef,
   196→		StartTime:  time.Now(),
   197→		NativeData: data,
   198→	}, nil
   199→}
   200→
   201→// WriteSession writes a session to Copilot's storage.
   202→func (c *CopilotAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   203→	if session == nil {
   204→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   208→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   212→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   216→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return nil
   224→}
   225→
   226→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Copilot session.
   227→func (c *CopilotAgent) FormatResumeCommand(_ string) string {
   228→	return "copilot --resume"
   229→}
   230→
```

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"bytes"
     7→	"context"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"os/exec"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// AgentNameClaudeCode is the name for Claude Code agent.
    17→const AgentNameClaudeCode = "claude-code"
    18→
    19→// AgentNameGemini is the name for Gemini CLI agent.
    20→const AgentNameGemini = "gemini"
    21→
    22→// AgentNameCopilot is the name for GitHub Copilot CLI agent.
    23→const AgentNameCopilot = "github-copilot"
    24→
    25→// AgentRunner abstracts invoking a coding agent for e2e tests.
    26→// This follows the multi-agent pattern from cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go.
    27→type AgentRunner interface {
    28→	// Name returns the agent name (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini-cli")
    29→	Name() string
    30→
    31→	// IsAvailable checks if the agent CLI is installed and authenticated
    32→	IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    33→
    34→	// RunPrompt executes a prompt and returns the result
    35→	RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    36→
    37→	// RunPromptWithTools executes with specific allowed tools
    38→	RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
    39→}
    40→
    41→// AgentResult holds the result of an agent invocation.
    42→type AgentResult struct {
    43→	Stdout   string
    44→	Stderr   string
    45→	ExitCode int
    46→	Duration time.Duration
    47→}
    48→
    49→// AgentRunnerConfig holds configuration for agent runners.
    50→type AgentRunnerConfig struct {
    51→	Model   string        // Model to use (e.g., "haiku" for Claude)
    52→	Timeout time.Duration // Timeout per prompt
    53→}
    54→
    55→// NewAgentRunner creates an agent runner based on the agent name.
    56→//
    57→//nolint:ireturn // factory pattern intentionally returns interface
    58→func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    59→	switch name {
    60→	case AgentNameClaudeCode:
    61→		return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    62→	case AgentNameGemini:
    63→		return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    64→	case AgentNameCopilot:
    65→		return NewCopilotRunner(config)
    66→	default:
    67→		// Return a runner that reports as unavailable
    68→		return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    69→	}
    70→}
    71→
    72→// unavailableRunner is returned for unknown agent names.
    73→type unavailableRunner struct {
    74→	name string
    75→}
    76→
    77→func (r *unavailableRunner) Name() string { return r.name }
    78→
    79→func (r *unavailableRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
    80→	return false, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", r.name)
    81→}
    82→
    83→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPrompt(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    84→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    85→}
    86→
    87→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPromptWithTools(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    88→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ClaudeCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for Claude Code CLI.
    92→type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    93→	Model        string
    94→	Timeout      time.Duration
    95→	AllowedTools []string
    96→}
    97→
    98→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    99→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {
   100→	model := config.Model
   101→	if model == "" {
   102→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL")
   103→		if model == "" {
   104→			model = "haiku"
   105→		}
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	timeout := config.Timeout
   109→	if timeout == 0 {
   110→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   111→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   112→				timeout = parsed
   113→			}
   114→		}
   115→		if timeout == 0 {
   116→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   117→		}
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	return &ClaudeCodeRunner{
   121→		Model:        model,
   122→		Timeout:      timeout,
   123→		AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"},
   124→	}
   125→}
   126→
   127→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) Name() string {
   128→	return AgentNameClaudeCode
   129→}
   130→
   131→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   132→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   133→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   134→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   135→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   136→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   137→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   138→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   142→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   143→	defer cancel()
   144→
   145→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   146→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   147→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return true, nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   154→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   155→}
   156→
   157→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   158→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   159→	args := []string{
   160→		"--model", r.Model,
   161→		"-p", prompt,
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   165→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   166→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   167→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   168→		args = append(args, tools...)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Create context with timeout
   172→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   173→	defer cancel()
   174→
   175→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   176→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   177→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   178→
   179→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   180→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   181→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   182→
   183→	start := time.Now()
   184→	err := cmd.Run()
   185→	duration := time.Since(start)
   186→
   187→	result := &AgentResult{
   188→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   189→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   190→		Duration: duration,
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   195→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   196→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   197→		} else {
   198→			result.ExitCode = -1
   199→		}
   200→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   201→		return result, err
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	result.ExitCode = 0
   205→	return result, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// GeminiCLIRunner implements AgentRunner for Gemini CLI.
   209→// See: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli
   210→type GeminiCLIRunner struct {
   211→	Model   string
   212→	Timeout time.Duration
   213→}
   214→
   215→// NewGeminiCLIRunner creates a new Gemini CLI runner with the given config.
   216→func NewGeminiCLIRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *GeminiCLIRunner {
   217→	model := config.Model
   218→	if model == "" {
   219→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_GEMINI_MODEL")
   220→		// Default to gemini-2.5-flash if not specified
   221→		if model == "" {
   222→			model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
   223→		}
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	timeout := config.Timeout
   227→	if timeout == 0 {
   228→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   229→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   230→				timeout = parsed
   231→			}
   232→		}
   233→		if timeout == 0 {
   234→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	return &GeminiCLIRunner{
   239→		Model:   model,
   240→		Timeout: timeout,
   241→	}
   242→}
   243→
   244→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) Name() string {
   245→	return AgentNameGemini
   246→}
   247→
   248→// IsAvailable checks if Gemini CLI is installed and authenticated.
   249→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   250→	// Check if gemini CLI is in PATH
   251→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("gemini"); err != nil {
   252→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Check if gemini is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   256→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   257→	defer cancel()
   258→
   259→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", "--version")
   260→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   261→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not working: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	return true, nil
   265→}
   266→
   267→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   268→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   269→}
   270→
   271→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   272→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   273→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   274→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   275→	args := []string{
   276→		"-m", r.Model,
   277→		"-p", prompt,
   278→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   282→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   283→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   284→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   285→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   286→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   287→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   291→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   292→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   293→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create context with timeout
   297→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   298→	defer cancel()
   299→
   300→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   301→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   302→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   303→
   304→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   305→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   306→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   307→
   308→	start := time.Now()
   309→	err := cmd.Run()
   310→	duration := time.Since(start)
   311→
   312→	result := &AgentResult{
   313→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   314→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   315→		Duration: duration,
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   320→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   321→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   322→		} else {
   323→			result.ExitCode = -1
   324→		}
   325→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   326→		return result, err
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	result.ExitCode = 0
   330→	return result, nil
   331→}
   332→
   333→// CopilotRunner implements AgentRunner for GitHub Copilot CLI.
   334→type CopilotRunner struct {
   335→	Model   string
   336→	Timeout time.Duration
   337→}
   338→
   339→// NewCopilotRunner creates a new Copilot CLI runner with the given config.
   340→func NewCopilotRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *CopilotRunner {
   341→	model := config.Model
   342→	if model == "" {
   343→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_COPILOT_MODEL")
   344→		if model == "" {
   345→			model = "claude-haiku-4.5"
   346→		}
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	timeout := config.Timeout
   350→	if timeout == 0 {
   351→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   352→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   353→				timeout = parsed
   354→			}
   355→		}
   356→		if timeout == 0 {
   357→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   358→		}
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	return &CopilotRunner{
   362→		Model:   model,
   363→		Timeout: timeout,
   364→	}
   365→}
   366→
   367→func (r *CopilotRunner) Name() string {
   368→	return AgentNameCopilot
   369→}
   370→
   371→// IsAvailable checks if Copilot CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   372→func (r *CopilotRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   373→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("copilot"); err != nil {
   374→		return false, fmt.Errorf("copilot CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   378→	defer cancel()
   379→
   380→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "copilot", "--version")
   381→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   382→		return false, fmt.Errorf("copilot CLI not working: %w", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	return true, nil
   386→}
   387→
   388→func (r *CopilotRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   389→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   390→}
   391→
   392→func (r *CopilotRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   393→	// Copilot uses --allow-all-tools for non-interactive mode which grants all permissions.
   394→	// The tools parameter is ignored because Copilot tool names differ from Claude Code's
   395→	// (e.g., "bash" vs "Bash", "create" vs "Write") and --available-tools restricts
   396→	// rather than allows, so passing Claude tool names would disable most tools.
   397→	args := []string{
   398→		"--model", r.Model,
   399→		"-p", prompt,
   400→		"--allow-all-tools",
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   404→	defer cancel()
   405→
   406→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   407→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "copilot", args...)
   408→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   409→
   410→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   411→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   412→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   413→
   414→	start := time.Now()
   415→	err := cmd.Run()
   416→	duration := time.Since(start)
   417→
   418→	result := &AgentResult{
   419→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   420→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   421→		Duration: duration,
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	if err != nil {
   425→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   426→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   427→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   428→		} else {
   429→			result.ExitCode = -1
   430→		}
   431→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   432→		return result, err
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	result.ExitCode = 0
   436→	return result, nil
   437→}
   438→
```

     2→
     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure
     6→type GeminiSettings struct {
     7→	HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig `json:"hooksConfig,omitempty"`
     8→	Hooks       GeminiHooks       `json:"hooks,omitempty"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// GeminiHooksConfig contains tool-related settings
    12→type GeminiHooksConfig struct {
    13→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled,omitempty"`
    14→}
    15→
    16→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations
    17→type GeminiHooks struct {
    18→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    19→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    20→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    24→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    25→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    26→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    27→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    28→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    29→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    33→type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    34→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    35→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.
    39→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini CLI requires a "name" field for each hook entry.
    40→type GeminiHookEntry struct {
    41→	Name    string `json:"name"`
    42→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    43→	Command string `json:"command"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd hooks
    47→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    48→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    49→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    50→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    51→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    52→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    53→	Source         string `json:"source,omitempty"` // For SessionStart: startup, resume, clear
    54→	Reason         string `json:"reason,omitempty"` // For SessionEnd: exit, logout
    55→}
    56→
    57→// agentHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeAgent/AfterAgent hooks.
    58→// BeforeAgent includes the user's prompt, similar to Claude's UserPromptSubmit.
    59→type agentHookInputRaw struct {
    60→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    62→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    63→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    64→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    65→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt,omitempty"` // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
    66→}
    67→
    68→// toolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeTool/AfterTool hooks
    69→type toolHookInputRaw struct {
    70→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    72→	Cwd            string          `json:"cwd"`
    73→	HookEventName  string          `json:"hook_event_name"`
    74→	Timestamp      string          `json:"timestamp"`
    75→	ToolName       string          `json:"tool_name"`
    76→	ToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:"tool_input"`
    77→	ToolResponse   json.RawMessage `json:"tool_response,omitempty"` // Only for AfterTool
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Tool names used in Gemini CLI that modify files
    81→// Note: Gemini CLI uses different names in different contexts:
    82→// - Internal/transcript names: write_file, replace
    83→// - Display names: WriteFile, Edit
    84→const (
    85→	ToolWriteFile = "write_file"
    86→	ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    87→	ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    88→	ToolReplace   = "replace"
    89→)
    90→
    91→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in Gemini CLI
    92→var FileModificationTools = []string{

    94→	ToolEditFile,
    95→	ToolSaveFile,
    96→	ToolReplace,
    97→}
    98→
    99→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.
   100→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message has a tokens object.
   101→type geminiMessageTokens struct {
   102→	Input    int `json:"input"`
   103→	Output   int `json:"output"`
   104→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
   105→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
   106→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`
   107→	Total    int `json:"total"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.
   111→// Used for extracting token counts from Gemini transcripts.
   112→type geminiMessageWithTokens struct {
   113→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   114→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   115→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   116→}
